Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the transfer object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 561. The moduli space has dimension at most 189 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on transfer structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 561. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 189. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the transfer structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 958. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
